# The Central Council of Homoeopathy in pursuance of clause a of sub section 1 and 2 of Section 21 and sub section 2 of Section 15 of the Homoeopathy Central Council Act

CENTRAL COUNCIL OF HOMOEOPATHY NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 21st June, 2021 No.13-32/2021-CCH/XXVI-CRH.— The Central Council of Homoeopathy in pursuance of clause (a) of sub-section (1), and (2) of Section 21 and sub-section (2) of Section 15 of the Homoeopathy Central Council Act, 1973 (59 of 1973) maintains a Register known as the Central Register of Homoeopathy. Part -I of said Register is being published in the Gazette of India for general information From S.No.1 to 20243, 20244 to 33097, 33098 to 37359, 37360 to 39961, 39962 to 45989, 45990 to 49450, 49451 to 61693, 61694 to 66678, 66679 to 72135, 72136 to 86959, 86960 to 100446, 100447 to 115139, 115140 to 129438, 129439 to 145398, 145399 to 147514, 147515 to 158079, 158080 to 164994, 164995 to 174034, 174035 to 186006 , 186007 to 198702 , 198703 to 204732 and 204733 to 206349 included in Part-I of Central Register of Homoeopathy published in the Gazette of India, Part-III, Section 4 dated January 2, 1993; October 9, 2004; May 26, 2007; November 15, 2008; August 8, 2009; April 24, 2010; April 16, 2011; March 3, 2012, August 24, 2013, February 22, 2014, October 4, 2014, May 25, 2015, August,19, 2017, March 17, 2018, August 21,2018, January 09, 2019, March 5, 2019, August 2, 2019, November19, 2019, July 22, 2020 , October 28, 2020 and March 25, 2021 respectively.

Dr. KUMAR VIVEKANAND, Registrar
